### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証し、UDPは保証しない。TCPは届いたことと順番を確認して再送するので確実だが遅れやすい。UDPは確認せず送るだけなので速くて軽いが、届かないことや順番が入れ替わることがある。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local antes de compartirla, mientras nadie más la usa. Usa merge para integrar ramas ya compartidas o publicadas, porque reescribir historial que otros tienen rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 保证数据可靠、按顺序送到（先建连接，丢了重传），UDP 不做这些保证，只管尽快发出去，换来低延迟和低开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to keep history linear before you open a PR or merge. Merge when the branch is shared or others have pulled it, because rebasing rewrites commits and causes conflicts for them.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用 `bun test` 后面直接跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

只写文件名的一部分也可以，Bun 会匹配路径包含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test parse
```

如果想只跑某一个测试用例（不是整个文件），用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "解析空字符串"
```

这三种可以组合，比如 `bun test parse -t "空字符串"`。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before that change is applied to the data files. If the system crashes midway, it replays the log on restart so committed work survives and half-finished work can be rolled back.
```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not run yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Writes per second and p99 latency</div>

<script>
function fmt(r){ return r.wps + " writes/s · p99 " + r.p99; }
function render(runs){
  runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
  var list = document.getElementById("runs");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  if (!runs.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">Nightly results will list here.</div>';
    return;
  }
  var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
  document.getElementById("latest").textContent = last.night + ": " + fmt(last);
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = "Compaction stalls: " + (last.stalls == null ? "not reported" : last.stalls);
  runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function(r){
    var row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
    row.firstChild.textContent = r.night;
    row.lastChild.textContent = fmt(r);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get("runs")).then(render);
if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get("runs")).then(render); });
</script>

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">北京出差 · 还没有花销</div>
  </div>

  <div id="days"></div>

  <details class="k-add" id="add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-card">
      <div class="k-field"><label>哪天</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11-23"></div>
      <div class="k-field"><label>花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="打车、午饭、酒店…"></div>
      <div class="k-field"><label>多少钱</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
      <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
      <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删掉这笔</button>
    </div>
  </details>
</main>

<script>
let items = [];
let editId = null;
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const fmt = n => '¥' + Number(n).toLocaleString('zh-CN', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });

function render() {
  const total = items.reduce((s, i) => s + Number(i.amount), 0);
   … i => i.id === r.dataset.id);
  if (!it) return;
  editId = it.id;
  $('day').value = it.day;
  $('what').value = it.what;
  $('amt').value = it.amount;
  $('save').textContent = '改好了';
  $('del').hidden = false;
  $('add').open = true;
};

$('save').onclick = async () => {
  const day = $('day').value.trim();
  const what = $('what').value.trim();
  const amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!day || !what || !(amount >= 0)) return;
  if (editId) {
    items = items.map(i => i.id === editId ? { ...i, day, what, amount } : i);
  } else {
    items.push({ id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), day, what, amount });
  }
  await ducter.set('items', items);
  reset();
  render();
};

$('del').onclick = async () => {
  if (!editId) return;
  items = items.filter(i => i.id !== editId);
  await ducter.set('items', items);
  reset();
  render();
};

async function load() {
  const v = await ducter.get('items');
  items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();

    const wrap=document.createElement("div");
    wrap.appendChild(main);wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(wrap);row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  const t=document.getElementById("total");
  const c=document.getElementById("count");
  if(xs.length===0){
    t.textContent="¥0";t.classList.add("k-none");
    c.textContent="还没记";
  }else{
    t.classList.remove("k-none");
    t.textContent="¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    c.textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
}
document.getElementById("add").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim();
  if(!what||!yuan)return;
  const xs=(ducter.get("expenses")||[]).slice();
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
  document.getElementById("day").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf with all four PRs, and you tick each one off as it lands.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1515 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
const items = ducter.get("items") || [];
let done = {};

function render() {
  const list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  items.forEach(it => {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    const on = !!done[it.id];
    row.innerHTML =
      '<div class="k-row-main"><div>' + (on ? '&#9745; ' : '&#9744; ') + it.title +
      '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + it.repo + ' · ' + it.sub + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + (on ? 'landed' : '') + '</div>';
    row.onclick = () => toggle(it.id);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  const landed = items.filter(it => done[it.id]).length;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent = String(items.length - landed);
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent =
    landed === 0 ? 'Tick each one off as it lands' : landed + ' landed';
  ducter.summary((items.length - landed) + ' waiting, ' + landed + ' landed');
}

async function toggle(id) {
  done[id] = !done[id];
  await ducter.set('done', done);
  render();
}

(async function init() {
  done = (await ducter.get('done')) || {};
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Mon"},{"id":"mem10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tue"},{"id":"ducter418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","sub":"Draft"}]}

<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="餐饮">餐饮</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="其他">其他</button></div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通";
function items(){return ducter.get("items")||[]}
function draw(){
  … oin(" · ");
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=sub;
    r.querySelector(".k-row-main").appendChild(s);
    r.querySelector(".k-row-value").textContent="¥"+Number(x.amt||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    r.onclick=()=>{ducter.ask("删掉这一笔（"+(x.name||x.trip)+"）？").then(ok=>{if(ok){const ys=items();ys.splice(i,1);ducter.set("items",ys)}})};
    list.appendChild(r);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN"):"还没记");
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.c;
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false"));
});
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat(document.getElementById("amt").value);
  if(!amt||amt<=0)return;
  const xs=items();
  xs.push({trip:document.getElementById("trip").value.trim(),name:document.getElementById("name").value.trim(),day:document.getElementById("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  document.getElementById("amt").value="";
  document.getElementById("name").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","name":"高铁 上海虹桥→北京南 G1","cat":"交通","day":"11/20","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差","name":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","cat":"住宿","day":"11/20–21","amt":1036}]}
  [also used tools] app
